```json
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"worker_id\": \"worker_ops_llama_signal_auc_cohens_d_20260517T021015\", \"status\": \"done\", \"task_type\": \"ops:llama_signal_auc_cohens_d\", \"project\": \"contamcal-irt-calibration\", \"started_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:10:15.545012+00:00\", \"ended_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:14:26.951719+00:00\", \"setup\": null, \"teardown\": null, \"setup_data\": {}, \"result\": {\"raw\": \"完成。结果保存在 `/root/autodl-tmp/exp008/llama_signal_analysis/`。\\n\\n## 关键发现\\n\\n### s1_embedding: 完全无效 (AUC = 0.5, Cohen's d = 0.0)\\ns1_embedding 对 Llama 跨所有dosage完全相同（值不变），对contamination无区分力。这是因为 embedding signal 基于固定参考模型计算，不随 SFT 训练改变。\\n\\n### s2_minkpp: 几乎无效（确认 Reviewer 预期）\\n- d000 vs d100: MMLU Cohen's d = 0.035, AUC = 0.508; GSM8K d = -0.338, AUC = 0.391; ARC d = 0.210, AUC = 0.557\\n- 除 ARC 外均接近随机，且方向不一致（有时contaminated更高有时更低）\\n\\n### s4_selfcritique: 唯一有区分力的 signal\\n- d000 vs d100: MMLU d = **-1.37**, AUC = 0.173 (即 flipped AUC = **0.827**); GSM8K d = **-1.27**, AUC = 0.187 (flipped = **0.813**); ARC d = **-2.40**, AUC = 0.057 (flipped = **0.943**)\\n- 注意 AUC < 0.5 说明 contaminated items 的 s4 值更**负**（lower self-critique score），flipped 后区分力很强\\n- d000 vs d025 也有明显效应：MMLU d = 0.75, GSM8K d = 1.05\\n\\n### 与 Qwen 对比\\nQwen exp-008 中 s1_embedding 和 s2_minkpp 在 Base regime 都有高 AUC (>0.95)，而 Llama 这两个 signal 完全失效。根本区别在于 Qwen 的 Base/SFT-clean/SFT-contam 是**不同模型类型**间比较，而 Llama 实验是**同一架构不同训练数据**的比较，s1_embedding 对后者不敏感。s4_selfcritique 是 Llama dosage 实验中唯一可用的contamination signal。\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me process the worker results. The worker completed an analysis of Llama signal AUC and Cohen's d. Key findings:

1. **s1_embedding**: Completely ineffective for Llama (AUC = 0.5, Cohen's d = 0.0) - because embedding signal is based on a fixed reference model and doesn't change with SFT training.

2. **s2_minkpp**: Nearly ineffective - AUC close to random for most benchmarks, inconsistent direction.

3. **s4_selfcritique**: The only discriminating signal for Llama:
   - d000 vs d100: MMLU flipped AUC = 0.827, GSM8K flipped = 0.813, ARC flipped = 0.943
   - d000 vs d025: Also shows strong effects (MMLU d=0.75, GSM8K d=1.05)

4. **vs Qwen comparison**: Qwen had high AUC for s1/s2 because it compared different model types (Base vs SFT), while Llama compares same architecture with different training data, making s1_embedding insensitive. This distinction is crucial for the cross-family IRT pooling approach.

For the cross-family model, only s4_selfcritique emerges as a reliable signal that works across both Qwen and Llama families, since s1_embedding and s2_minkpp are effective for Qwen's base-to-SFT comparison but fail to capture Llama's within-SFT dosage variations. This finding directly impacts the D123 decision about which signals to include in the primary 2PL+γ model, and it connects to the paper's framing around domain-differential contamination effects and the roadmap's cross-family sample size constraints.

Now I'm checking the registry to identify which specific experiments generated this analysis and then I'll need to update the report with these findings and discuss the implications with the Director.
```

RRF 脚本 SKIP_PATTERNS 已包含 \\\"old_alpha16\\\"\\n\\n## d025 RRF 时序教训 (2026-05-17)\\n\\n**问题**: 第一次 d025 RRF 在 s4 merge 前 12 分钟就跑了，导致 Llama MMLU 只用了 2/3 signals。Worker 输出到 rrf_d025_v2/ 新目录而非覆盖原路径，导致原文件时间戳不变。\\n**教训**: RRF 重跑时必须先 rm 旧文件再生成，且验证时间戳确认更新。\\n\\n## 提交规则 (2026-05-16, Director 强制)\\n\\n所有 signal/eval 实验提交前必须：\\n1. `ls $OUTPUT_DIR` 检查是否已有完整产出（避免重复计算浪费 GPU）\\n2. 命令强制携带 `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1`（避免 HF Hub 网络重试卡死进程）\\n\", \"total_chars\": 1762, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
